Implement:
def project_feasible(x, n):
  x=x.copy()
  # ensure lengths
  target=n/2.0
  # First clip to [0,1]
  x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  # binary search shift
  if x.sum()==target return x
  lo=-x.max() # makes all >=0? if t=lo then min + lo=0
  hi=1-x.min()
  # f(t)=sum(clip(x+t,0,1)) - target
  for 60:
    mid=(lo+hi)/2; val=np.clip(x+mid,0,1).sum()-target
    if val>0: hi=mid else lo=mid
  y=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
  diff=target-y.sum()
  # distribute diff to unsaturated (0<y<1) or all with room
  mask=(y>1e-15)&(y<1-1e-15)
  if mask.sum()>0: y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum(); y=np.clip(y,0,1)
  else: # all saturated? if sum mismatch due to target impossible? target=n/2, if all 0/1 sum integer maybe target integer for n even; okay; adjust some.
  # Finally exact by adjusting largest room coordinate
  # We need sum exact for evaluator? Floating; can enforce by adding diff to coordinates not at bounds.
  diff=target-y.sum()
  for _ in range(3):
    if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    # choose coordinate with room in direction
    if diff>0: idx=np.where(y<1-1e-12)[0]
    else: idx=np.where(y>1e-12)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: break
    # add to all or one? To minimize distortion add to all with room in small diff
    y[idx]+=diff/len(idx)
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    diff=target-y.sum()
  # final one correction if diff and room
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
    if diff>0:
      # pick idx with largest room
      i=int(np.argmax(1-y)); y[i]=min(1,y[i]+diff)
    else:
      i=int(np.argmin(y)); y[i]=max(0,y[i]+diff)
  return y
This ensures exact sum within float maybe.

Compute pos/neg arrays:
def compute_g(h,n):
  ones = 1-h
  gpos = np.empty(n)
  gneg = np.empty(n)
  # using loop dot slices
  for k in range(n):
    m=n-k
    gpos[k]=np.dot(h[:m], ones[k:k+m])
  for k in range(1,n):
    m=n-k
    gneg[k]=np.dot(h[k:k+m], ones[:m])
  return gpos, gneg
This uses Python loop 2n dots. For n=250, 500 dots. Each dot length avg 125 C-level; total 62k mult? Actually each pair duplicated; 2*31k=62k. Plus loop overhead 500. Could be ~0.3ms? fine. For n=500: 1000 dots avg250 ->250k mult? Wait sum m = n(n+1)/2 ≈125k per pos; times 2 =250k. Still okay. For n=1000: ~1M? pos 500k, neg 500k total 1M. Good.
Can optimize with FFT if n large but dot fine for <=1000 maybe. Python 1000 loop per eval maybe overhead maybe 0.002s. 10k evals=20s. Good.

Gradient for one lag:
def grad_pos(k,h):
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  m=n-k
  # x_i x_{i+k}; derivative wrt x_i: 1-x_{i+k}; wrt x_{i+k}: -x_i
  grad[:m] += (1-h[k:k+m])
  grad[k:k+m] -= h[:m]
 return grad

def grad_neg(k,h): # ∑ h[i+k](1-h[i])
  m=n-k
  # derivative wrt h[i+k]: 1-h[i]
  grad[k:k+m] += 1-h[:m]
  # derivative wrt h[i]: -h[i+k]
  grad[:m] -= h[k:k+m]
 return grad
For signed lag: we can store (kind,k). For top lags, sum weighted gradient. Note pos[0]=neg[0]; use once.

Let's derive aggregation efficiently using our pos/neg definitions:
For each pos lag k: weight wp[k]; grad += wp*( (1-h[k:]) extended - h[:-k] extended ). For neg lag k>0: weight wn[k]; grad += wn*( (1-h[:n-k]) placed at k: -? We have formulas: grad_pos: grad[:m]+=1-h[k:]; grad[k:]-=h[:m]. grad_neg: grad[k:]+=1-h[:m]; grad[:m]-=h[k:].

def minimize_pair(h, i, j, current_c, max_evals=24):
    # interval for δ moving from j to i: h_i += δ, h_j -= δ
    lo = max(-h[i], h[j]-1)  # since h_j>=0 => -δ >= -h_j => δ <= h_j; h_j<=1 => -δ <=1-h_j => δ >= h_j-1
    hi = min(1-h[i], h[j])
    if hi <= lo + 1e-15: return h,current_c
    # golden section on [lo,hi] for convex f; but f may have minimum at 0? We know 0 inside.
```

def eval_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))

Algorithm `top_gradient_refine`:
```
def top_gradient_refine(h,n,budget, top_k=32, step=0.05):
  best...
  for iter:
    if time>budget break
    pos,neg=compute_gpos_neg(h)
    vals = concatenate [pos[0], pos[1:], neg[1:]]
    c = max(vals)*dx
    if c<best: update best, store h
    # sort descending top_k
    order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:top_k]
    # gradient zeros; for each idx: if idx==0 pos lag0; else determine pos/neg
    grad = active_gradient(order,h,n)  # avg
    # line search: F_candidate = average of vals at candidate? Use exact C for best; for step use directional decrease:
    # Compute current F = average vals[order]
    Fcur=np.mean(vals[order])
    h_new=NULL
    for scale in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.031]:
       h_try=project(h - scale*step*grad, n)
       pos,neg=compute_gpos_neg(h_try)
       vals2=...
       # To ensure progress on original top? Use C eval maybe cheap: c_try=eval_c5(h_try)
       # acceptance if c_try < current_c or F2 < Fcur - tol? Use both: if c_try < best_c? But may cycle. Accept if c_try < c - 1e-13 or F2 < Fcur -1e-10.
       if c_try < c - 1e-13 or np.mean(vals2[order]) < Fcur -1e-8:
          accept; break
    if not accept:
       step*0.7; if step<1e-6 break
    else: h=h_try; maybe step*=1.05
```
`active_gradient(order,h,n)` loop over top lags. Need mapping from order to lag: Build arrays `lag_types` and `lag_inds` for vals: index 0 -> ('pos',0); 1..n-1 -> ('pos',k); n..2n-2 -> ('neg', k = idx-n+1). But computing vals each iter: vals = np.empty(2n-1); vals[0]=pos[0]; vals[1:n]=pos[1:]; vals[n:]=neg[1:]. Gradient: for lag (k): pos: grad[:m]+=1-h[k:]; grad[k:]-=h[:m]; neg: grad[k:]+=1-h[:m]; grad[:m]-=h[k:]. Sum over top, divide by top_k.
This is O(top_k*n) ~8k; okay.
Projection exact.

Implement FFT-based C for larger n:
```
def correlate_c5_fft(h):
    n=len(h)
    # use scipy.signal.fftconvolve? Need cross-correlation full.
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
    # r[k] = sum h[i] (1-h[i+k])? Could compute np.convolve? Maybe use scipy.signal.correlate.
    c = correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full', method='fft')
    return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

Projection exact:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target = n/2.0
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # shift binary search
    if abs(h.sum()-target) < 1e-12:
        # final fix
        h = np.clip(h,0,1)
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if diff>0:
           idx=np.where(h<1-1e-12)[0]
        else:
           idx=np.where(h>1e-12)[0]
        if len(idx)>0:
           h[idx]+=diff/len(idx)
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
        return h
    lo = -float(np.max(h))
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(h))
    f = target
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       val=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
       if val > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    y=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-y.sum()
    mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
    if mask.any():
       y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    # fix residual
    for _ in range(3):
       diff=target-y.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(y<1-1e-12)[0]
       else:
          idx=np.where(y>1e-12)[0]
       if len(idx)==0: break
       # if adding to many may clip; adjust room total
       room = np.abs(idx)??
```
Simpler exact fix: after binary search and distribute to mask, residual may be tiny due clipping. We can adjust one variable:
```
for _ in range(5):
 diff=target-y.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
 if diff>0:
    # choose variable with largest room
    candidates=np.where(y<1.0)[0]
    if len(candidates)==0: break
    i=candidates[np.argmax(1-y[candidates])]
    y[i]=min(1.0, y[i]+diff)
 else:
    candidates=np.where(y>0.0)[0]
    if len(candidates)==0: break
    i=candidates[np.argmin(y[candidates])]
    y[i]=max(0.0, y[i]+diff)
# final if still due all clipped, distribute to not all? target feasible.
```
This might alter a variable beyond 1? We cap; if diff > room of one, loop. Good.

Compute pos/neg:
```
def compute_gpos_neg(h):
    n=len(h)
    one=1.0-h
    pos=np.empty(n); neg=np.empty(n)
    pos[0]=np.dot(h, one)
    for k in range(1,n):
        m=n-k
        pos[k]=np.dot(h[:m], one[k:])
    neg[0]=pos[0]
    for k in range(1,n):
        m=n-k
        neg[k]=np.dot(h[k:], one[:m])
    return pos,neg
```
For `active_gradient(order,h,n)`, need mapping. We can build vals and order; pass order where values index. But for gradient need lag type.
```
def lag_info(idx,n):
   if idx < n: return 0, idx  # pos k=idx (idx 0..n-1)
   else: return 1, idx - n + 1 # neg k=1..n-1
```
Then:
```
def active_gradient(order,h,n, top_k):
    n=len(h); m=n; g=np.zeros(n); one=1-h
    for idx in order[:top_k]:
       if idx < n:
          k=idx; m=n-k
          if m>0:
            g[:m] += one[k:]
            g[k:] -= h[:m]
       else:
          k=idx-n+1
          if k<n:
            m=n-k
            g[k:] += one[:m]
            g[:m] -= h[k:]
    return g/top_k
```
For k=0, both formula same? idx=0 pos m=n: g[:n]+=one; g[0:]-=h -> g=1-2h. derivative of h(1-h) yes.
Note order top indices may contain duplicates? vals length unique; no.

def pair_try(h,i,j,n,max_move,eval_max=18):
    hi0=h[i]; hj0=h[j]
    lo = max(-hi0, hj0-1.0, -max_move)
    hi = min(1.0-hi0, hj0, max_move)
    if hi - lo < 1e-10: return None
    base=h.copy()
    # Possibly endpoints: 0 already base, but golden will evaluate.
    a=lo; b=hi
    invphi=(5**0.5-1)/2
    c = b - invphi*(b-a)
    d = a + invphi*(b-a)
    base[i]=hi0+c; base[j]=hj0-c; fc=eval_c5(base); best_d=c; best_c=fc
    base[i]=hi0+d; base[j]=hj0-d; fd=eval_c5(base); if fd<best_c: best_d=d; best_c=fd
    for _ in range(16):
       if b-a < 1e-5: break
       if fc < fd:
          b=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=b-invphi*(b-a); base[i]=hi0+c; base[j]=hj0-c; fc=eval_c5(base)
       else:

    if best_c < c0 - tol:
        base[i]=hi0+best_d; base[j]=hj0-best_d
        return base, best_c
    return None

Pair local search likely more important. Let's flesh it.

```
def pair_local_search(h,n,deadline, max_move=0.25, pairs_per_grad=120, max_eval_per_pair=14):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed?) # random at top-level? Need seed. We'll create global? But functions may need rng. Pass as argument or use np.random.
    no_imp=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
       pos,neg=compute_gpos_neg(best_h)
       vals=pack; c=max*dx
       if c<best_c: update
       if no_imp > 200 maybe break? add random.
       grad=active_gradient(top order,best_h,n)
       pairs = choose_pairs(grad,n,rng,top_count=80, rand_count=40)
       improved=False
       for i,j in pairs:
           if time.time()>deadline-5: break
           res = pair_try(best_h, i,j,n, best_c, max_move, max_eval_per_pair)
           if res is not None:
               hv,hc=res
               # project to fix sum
               hv=project_feasible(hv,n)
               hc=eval_c5(hv)
               if hc < best_c - 1e-14:

`choose_pairs`:
```
def choose_pairs(grad,n,rng,n_top=80,n_rand=40):
   if n<2: return []
   iu,ju = np.triu_indices(n,1)
   diff = np.abs(grad[iu] - grad[ju])
   pairs=[]
   if n_top < len(diff):
      part = np.argpartition(diff, -n_top)[-n_top:]
      # shuffle part
      rng.shuffle(part)
      pairs = list(zip(iu[part], ju[part]))
   else:
      pairs = list(zip(iu,ju))
   if n_rand:
      m=len(diff)
      ridx=rng.choice(m, size=min(n_rand,m), replace=False)
      for r in ridx: pairs.append((iu[r],ju[r]))
   # optional shuffle all
   rng.shuffle(pairs)
   return pairs
```
Creating triu_indices each call expensive? n=250, 31k; okay. For high n 500, 125k, okay. Could precompute list of pair indices? Top-level function cannot store mutable? We can cache in global dictionary? But no closures. Use module-level variable? Simpler each call.
`argpartition` on 31k each gradient; if many regrad, okay. We can recompute grad every sweep and choose list once. In while, after no improvement, recompute. So okay.

def eval_c5(h, force_direct=True? no):
   n=len(h)
   if n <= 450:
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
   else:
       c=correlate_fft(h)
   return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

def correlate_fft(h):
   n=len(h)
   N=1
   while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
   H=np.rfft(h,N); Y=np.rfft(1.0-h,N)
   r=np.irfft(H * np.conj(Y), N)
   # r[0] corresponds? It gives circular correlation aligned start? Need linear full length. For `scipy.signal.correlate` simpler.
```

Let's test conceptual code for pair line search golden. Need careful with variables names. We'll write top-level:
```
def pair_try(h, i, j, n, max_move=0.25, max_evals=16, tol=1e-10):
    hi0 = float(h[i]); hj0 = float(h[j])
    lo = max(-hi0, hj0 - 1.0, -max_move)
    hi = min(1.0 - hi0, hj0, max_move)
    if hi - lo < 1e-9:
        return None
    base = h.copy()
    base[i] = hi0; base[j]=hj0
    c0 = eval_c5(base)
    # quick endpoint? not needed.
    inv = (math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    a=lo; b=hi
    c = b - inv*(b-a)
    d = a + inv*(b-a)
    best_d=0.0; best_c=c0
    # eval c and d
    base[i]=hi0+c; base[j]=hj0-c; fc=eval_c5(base)
    if fc < best_c: best_c=fc; best_d=c
    base[i]=hi0+d; base[j]=hj0-d; fd=eval_c5(base)
    if fd < best_c: best_c=fd; best_d=d
    evals=2
    while b-a > 1e-6 and evals < max_evals:
       if fc < fd:
          b=d; d=c; fd=fc
          c=b-inv*(b-a)
          base[i]=hi0+c; base[j]=hj0-c; fc=eval_c5(base); evals+=1
          if fc < best_c: best_c=fc; best_d=c
       else:
          a=c; c=d; fc=fd
          d=a+inv*(b-a)
          base[i]=hi0+d; base[j]=hj0-d; fd=eval_c5(base); evals+=1
          if fd < best_c: best_c=fd; best_d=d
    # final midpoint
    mid=0.5*(a+b)
    base[i]=hi0+mid; base[j]=hj0-mid; cm=eval_c5(base)
    if cm < best_c: best_c=cm; best_d=mid
    if best_c < c0 - tol:
        base[i]=hi0+best_d; base[j]=hj0-best_d
        # final eval
        cf=eval_c5(base)
        if cf < c0 - tol:
            return base, cf
    return None
```
This may call eval more than max_evals? Start 2, loop maybe, final. Set max_evals 16.
If optimum at boundary and min beyond max_move, we may not find full but will step to boundary; repeated sweeps can move further if beneficial.

`pack_vals(pos,neg)`:
```
def pack_vals(pos,neg):
    vals=np.empty(2*len(pos)-1)
    vals[0]=pos[0]
    vals[1:len(pos)]=pos[1:]
    vals[len(pos):]=neg[1:]
    return vals
```

import time, math
import numpy as np
try:
    from scipy.signal import correlate as _sp_correlate
except Exception:
    _sp_correlate=None

def eval_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n <= 450 or _sp_correlate is None:
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    else:
        c = _sp_correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full', method='fft')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h,n): ...
def compute_gpos_neg(h): ...
def pack_vals(pos,neg): ...
def active_gradient(order,h,n): ...
def top_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,rng=top_rng? cannot default mutable? We can default None and create? But run passes rng.
```

`top_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,rng=None,top_k=24,step=0.05)`:
- Use eval_c5 and compute g. Use exact C for accept. We need `c_current`; if h maybe projected.
```
best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h)
c_cur=best_c
step=0.05; fail=0
while time.time() < deadline:
    tloc=time.time()
    pos,neg=compute_gpos_neg(h)
    vals=pack_vals(pos,neg)
    c_cur=float(np.max(vals)*(2.0/n))
    if c_cur < best_c: best_c=c_cur; best_h=h.copy()
    if c_cur <= 0.38079 and best_c <=0.38079: # maybe still polish but can break? no
    order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:top_k]
    grad=active_gradient(order,h,n)
    gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
    if gnorm <1e-10: break
    direction = -grad/gnorm
    accepted=False
    for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
        h_try=project_feasible(h + step*scale*direction, n)
        c_try=eval_c5(h_try)
        if c_try < c_cur - 1e-12:
            h=h_try; c_cur=c_try; accepted=True
            if c_try<best_c: best_c=c_try; best_h=h.copy()
            break
    if not accepted:
        step *=0.75
        fail +=1
        if step < 1e-6 or fail>80: break
        if fail % 20 == 0:
            # random small kick from best_h? Use h not best to explore; but best kept.
            h = project_feasible(best_h + 0.05*rng.randn(n), n)
            c_cur = eval_c5(h)
            if c_cur < best_c: update
            fail=0
    else:
        fail=0
        step = min(0.12, step*1.05)
    # if one iteration >0.5s, maybe stop? no
return project_feasible(best_h,n), best_c
```

`choose_pairs(grad,n,rng,n_top,n_rand)`:
```
def choose_pairs(grad,n,rng,n_top=80,n_rand=40):
    if n<2 or rng is None: return []
    iu,ju=np.triu_indices(n,1)
    diff=np.abs(grad[iu]-grad[ju])
    out=[]
    m=len(diff)
    if n_top >= m:
       top_idx=np.arange(m)
    else:
       top_idx=np.argpartition(diff, -n_top)[-n_top:]
    rng.shuffle(top_idx)
    for idx in top_idx: out.append((int(iu[idx]), int(ju[idx])))
    if n_rand>0 and m:
       rsize=min(n_rand,m)
       ridx=rng.choice(m, size=rsize, replace=False)
       for idx in ridx: out.append((int(iu[idx]), int(ju[idx])))
    rng.shuffle(out)
    return out
```
This creates triu and diff each call. Pair search may call many refresh; for n=250 okay. If long, okay.

def binary_anneal(h_start,n,deadline,rng=None):
    bits=np.zeros(n,bool); top n/2 ones from start (if h_start continuous) or convert to 0/1 by >0.5 and then adjust count.
    def ensure_bits(bits): if sum not target, adjust: if too many ones, set largest h? random ones to 0; if too few, etc. We can pass binary candidate already.
    cur=bits.copy(); cur_c=eval_c5(cur)
    best=bits.copy(); best_c=cur_c
    T=0.02 # C units
    minT=1e-6
    cool=0.9995
    last_improve=0
    for it in range(300000):
       if time>deadline break
       if it %1000? no
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       while cur[i]==cur[j]: j=rng.randint(n)
       cur[i],cur[j]=cur[j],cur[i]
       c=eval_c5(cur)
       if c < best_c - 1e-12:
          best=cur.copy(); best_c=c; last_improve=it

          cur_c=c

          cur[i],cur[j]=cur[j],cur[i]; # revert

       if T > minT: T *= cool

             cur=best.copy(); cur_c=best_c
             T=max(T,0.005)
    # local binary descent until deadline-2: random swaps only if improve
    improve_count=0
    while time<deadline-1:
       i,j random diff; swap; c; if c<best_c: best=cur.copy? Actually maintain cur? For descent, use cur.

Let's write code functions carefully.

Detailed code:
```
import time, math
import numpy as np
try:
    from scipy.signal import correlate as _signal_correlate
except Exception:
    _signal_correlate = None

def eval_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 450 or _signal_correlate is None:
        corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    else:
        corr = _signal_correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full', method='fft')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))
```
Potential: `scipy.signal.correlate` method='fft' for mode full may return smaller length? It does full. Good.

`project_feasible` robust. We need target `n*0.5` not `n/2` integer? Use float.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12:
       return _fix_sum(h,target)
    lo=-float(np.max(h)); hi=1.0-float(np.min(h))
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       val=np.clip(h+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if val > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    y=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    return _fix_sum(y,target)
```
`_fix_sum` top-level:
```
def _fix_sum(h,target):
   diff=target - float(h.sum())
   for _ in range(5):
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         idx=np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
      else:
         idx=np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
      if len(idx)==0: break
      # If distribute to all equal may clip; add fraction of min room
      if diff>0:
         room=1.0 - h[idx]
      else:
         room=h[idx]
      total_room=room.sum()
      if total_room <= 1e-15: break
      add=diff if abs(diff) <= total_room else (total_room/len(idx) if diff>0 else -total_room/len(idx))? Simpler: add = diff/len(idx) if abs(diff)/len(idx) < room.min()*0.9 else sign*room.min()
      # To avoid loop, add scaled to min(room, maxadd)
```
If diff large, distributing evenly could clip. Use iterative with clipping:
```
room = (1-h[idx]) if diff>0 else h[idx]
max_add = room.min()
add = diff/len(idx)
if add > max_add: add = max_add
if add < -max_add: add = -max_add
h[idx] += add
h=np.clip(h,0,1)
diff=target-h.sum()
```
Repeat until diff small. Finally single coordinate to exact:
```
if abs(diff)>1e-14:
   if diff>0:
      i=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); room=1-h[i]; h[i]=min(1,h[i]+diff)
   else: i=int(np.argmin(h)); h[i]=max(0,h[i]+diff)
```
This may not fully fix if room insufficient, but loop handles. Return clip.
Need ensure target feasible (it is). For all zeros/ones target maybe n/2; if diff but no room due clip, error but unlikely.

`compute_gpos_neg`: optimize by prealloc? use local variables for speed.
```
def compute_gpos_neg(h):
    n=int(len(h))
    one=1.0-h
    pos=np.empty(n,float); neg=np.empty(n,float)
    pos[0]=float(np.dot(h,one))
    for k in range(1,n):
       m=n-k
       pos[k]=float(np.dot(h[:m], one[k:]))
    neg[0]=pos[0]
    for k in range(1,n):
       m=n-k
       neg[k]=float(np.dot(h[k:], one[:m]))
    return pos,neg
```
`pack_vals`.

for idx in order:
   if idx < n:
      k=int(idx); m=n-k
      if m>0:
         g[:m] += one[k:]
         g[k:] -= h[:m]
   else:
      k=int(idx-n+1)
      if k>0:
         m=n-k
         g[k:] += one[:m]
         g[:m] -= h[k:]
```

def binary_anneal(h_start,n,deadline,rng=None,cool=0.9995,T0=0.02):
   if n%2 or rng is None: return None
   bits=make_bits; cur=bits.copy(); cur_c=eval_c5(cur)
   best=cur.copy(); best_c=cur_c
   T=T0; minT=1e-6; last=0
   max_iters=500000
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.time() >= deadline: break
      i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
      # ensure different bits; if many tries, break pair? use while up to10
      tries=0
      while cur[i]==cur[j] and tries<10:
         j=int(rng.randint(n)); tries+=1
      if cur[i]==cur[j]: continue
      cur[i],cur[j]=cur[j],cur[ i]  # swap
      c=eval_c5(cur)
      if c < best_c - 1e-12:
          best=cur.copy(); best_c=c; last=it
      if c <= cur_c + T:
          cur_c=c
      else:
          cur[i],cur[j]=cur[j],cur[i]
      if T > minT: T = max(minT, T*cool)
      if it - last > 50000:
          # random restart from best with a few perturb swaps
          cur=best.copy(); cur_c=best_c
          T=max(T,0.01)
          for _ in range(5): random swap accept if not worse? maybe
```

High-res: `upsample_h(h,n,nh)`:
```
def upsample_h(h,n,nh):
    if n==nh: return h.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    x_new=(np.arange(nh)+0.5)*(2.0/nh)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
```

Detailed implementation of each function:

`_fix_sum`:
```
def _fix_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-15)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-15)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
        else:
            room = h[idx]
        maxstep = float(np.min(room))
        step = diff / float(idx.size)
        if step > maxstep:
            step = maxstep
        elif step < -maxstep:
            step = -maxstep
        h[idx] += step
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            candidates = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
            if candidates.size:
                i = candidates[int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[candidates]))]
                add = min(1.0 - h[i], diff)
                h[i] += add
        else:
            candidates = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
            if candidates.size:
                i = candidates[int(np.argmin(h[candidates]))]
                add = max(-h[i], diff)
                h[i] += add
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
```
If step = maxstep but diff remains, loop. If idx includes values exactly 1, room 0; min room could be 0 causing step 0 if all room 0. But diff >0 and h<1-1e-15 ensures room>1e-15. Good.

def binary_prepare_bits(h,n):
   bits=np.zeros(n,bool)
   target=n//2
   if n%2==0 and target*2==n:
      idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
      bits[idx[:target]]=True
   else:
      # if odd, choose floor and maybe adjust? return None? We'll only call even.
   # Ensure sum target (even)
   s=int(bits.sum())
   while s<target:
       idx=np.where(bits==False)[0]
       if idx.size==0: break
       pick=int(rng?) No rng here. Could adjust based on h: choose highest h among False.
       pick=idx[np.argmax(h[idx])]
       bits[pick]=True; s+=1
   while s>target: ...
   return bits
```

def finalize(h,n):
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   # ensure exact sum
   target=0.5*n
   diff=target - h.sum()
   if diff>0:
      idx=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
   else: idx=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
   if idx.size:
      if diff>0: i=idx[np.argmax(1-h[idx])]
      else: i=idx[np.argmin(h[idx])]
      h[i]=np.clip(h[i]+diff,0,1)
   corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
   c=float(np.max(corr)*(2.0/n))
   return h,c
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.signal import correlate as _sp_correlate
except Exception:
    _sp_correlate = None


def finalize(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = _fix_sum(h, target)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    c = float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))
    return h, c


def _fix_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    for _ in range(6):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-15)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-15)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
        else:
            room = h[idx]
        if room.size == 0:
            break
        maxstep = float(np.min(room))
        step = diff / float(idx.size)
        if step > maxstep:
            step = maxstep
        elif step < -maxstep:
            step = -maxstep
        h[idx] += step
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            cand = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
            if cand.size:
                i = cand[int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[cand]))]
                h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + diff)
        else:
            cand = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
            if cand.size:
                i = cand[int(np.argmin(h[cand]))]
                h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + diff)
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = h[:n]
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
        return _fix_sum(h, target)

    lo = -float(np.max(h))
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(h))
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if val > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    y = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _fix_sum(y, target)


def eval_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 450 or _sp_correlate is None:
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    else:
        c = _sp_correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full', method='fft')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def compute_gpos_neg(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    one = 1.0 - h
    pos = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    neg = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    pos[0] = float(np.dot(h, one))
    neg[0] = pos[0]
    for k in range(1, n):
        m = n - k
        pos[k] = float(np.dot(h[:m], one[k:]))
        neg[k] = float(np.dot(h[k:], one[:m]))
    return pos, neg


def pack_vals(pos, neg):
    n = len(pos)
    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    vals[0] = pos[0]
    vals[1:n] = pos[1:]
    vals[n:] = neg[1:]
    return vals


def active_gradient(order, h, n):
    n = int(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    one = 1.0 - h
    cnt = 0
    for idx in order:
        idx = int(idx)
        if idx < n:
            k = idx
            m = n - k
            if m > 0:
                g[:m] += one[k:]
                g[k:] -= h[:m]
                cnt += 1
        else:
            k = idx - n + 1
            if 0 < k < n:
                m = n - k
                g[k:] += one[:m]
                g[:m] -= h[k:]
                cnt += 1
    if cnt > 0:
        g /= float(cnt)
    return g


def top_gradient_refine(h, n, deadline, rng=None, top_k=24, step=0.05):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h)
    c_cur = best_c
    step = float(step)
    fail = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        pos, neg = compute_gpos_neg(h)
        vals = pack_vals(pos, neg)
        order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:top_k]
        grad = active_gradient(order, h, n)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-12:
            break

        c_cur = eval_c5(h)
        if c_cur < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c_cur
            best_h = h.copy()

        direction = -grad / gnorm
        accepted = False
        for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            h_try = project_feasible(h + step * scale * direction, n)
            c_try = eval_c5(h_try)
            if c_try < c_cur - 1e-13:
                h = h_try
                c_cur = c_try
                if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = c_try
                    best_h = h.copy()
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            step *= 0.75
            fail += 1
            if step < 1e-7 or fail > 120:
                break
            if fail % 25 == 0:
                kick = project_feasible(0.85 * best_h + 0.15 * (best_h + 0.05 * rng.randn(n)), n)
                ck = eval_c5(kick)
                if ck < best_c + 0.01:
                    h = kick
                    c_cur = ck
                if c_cur < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = c_cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                fail = 0
        else:
            fail = 0
            step = min(0.12, step * 1.05)

    return project_feasible(best_h, n), best_c


def pair_try(h, i, j, n, max_move=0.25, max_evals=14, c0=None):
    n = int(n)
    if i == j or i < 0 or j < 0 or i >= n or j >= n:
        return None
    max_evals = max(6, int(max_evals))
    hi0 = float(h[i])
    hj0 = float(h[j])

    lo = max(-hi0, hj0 - 1.0, -max_move)
    hi = min(1.0 - hi0, hj0, max_move)
    if hi - lo < 1e-11:
        return None

    base = h.copy()
    base[i] = hi0
    base[j] = hj0
    if c0 is None:
        c0 = eval_c5(base)

    invphi = 0.5 * (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0)
    a = float(lo)
    b = float(hi)
    c = b - invphi * (b - a)
    d = a + invphi * (b - a)

    best_d = 0.0
    best_c = float(c0)

    base[i] = hi0 + c
    base[j] = hj0 - c
    fc = eval_c5(base)
    if fc < best_c:
        best_c = fc
        best_d = c

    base[i] = hi0 + d
    base[j] = hj0 - d
    fd = eval_c5(base)
    if fd < best_c:
        best_c = fd
        best_d = d

    evals = 2
    while (b - a) > 1e-8 and evals < max_evals:
        if fc < fd:
            b = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = b - invphi * (b - a)
            base[i] = hi0 + c
            base[j] = hj0 - c
            fc = eval_c5(base)
            evals += 1
            if fc < best_c:
                best_c = fc
                best_d = c
        else:
            a = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = a + invphi * (b - a)
            base[i] = hi0 + d
            base[j] = hj0 - d
            fd = eval_c5(base)
            evals += 1
            if fd < best_c:
                best_c = fd
                best_d = d

    mid = 0.5 * (a + b)
    base[i] = hi0 + mid
    base[j] = hj0 - mid
    cm = eval_c5(base)
    if cm < best_c:
        best_c = cm
        best_d = mid

    if best_c < float(c0) - 1e-11:
        base[i] = hi0 + best_d
        base[j] = hj0 - best_d
        cf = eval_c5(base)
        if cf < float(c0) - 1e-11:
            return base, cf
    return None


def choose_pairs(grad, n, rng, n_top=80, n_rand=40):
    n = int(n)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    if n < 2:
        return []
    iu, ju = np.triu_indices(n, 1)
    diff = np.abs(grad[iu] - grad[ju])
    m = diff.size
    if m == 0:
        return []

    out = []
    if n_top >= m:
        top_idx = np.arange(m)
    else:
        top_idx = np.argpartition(diff, -n_top)[-n_top:]
    top_idx = np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int)
    rng.shuffle(top_idx)
    for rr in top_idx:
        out.append((int(iu[rr]), int(ju[rr])))

    if n_rand > 0:
        rsize = min(int(n_rand), m)
        if rsize > 0:
            ridx = rng.choice(m, size=rsize, replace=False)
            for rr in ridx:
                out.append((int(iu[rr]), int(ju[rr])))

    rng.shuffle(out)
    return out


def pair_local_search(h, n, deadline, rng=None, max_move=0.25, max_evals=14,
                      pairs_per_grad=80, random_count=36):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    n = int(n)
    cur = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    cur_c = eval_c5(cur)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_c = cur_c
    no_imp = 0
    top_k = min(32, 2 * n - 1)

    while time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        pos, neg = compute_gpos_neg(cur)
        vals = pack_vals(pos, neg)
        order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:top_k]
        grad = active_gradient(order, cur, n)
        cur_c = eval_c5(cur)
        if cur_c < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c = cur_c
            best = cur.copy()

        improved_any = False
        accepted = 0
        pairs = choose_pairs(grad, n, rng, n_top=max(10, pairs_per_grad), n_rand=random_count)
        for ii, jj in pairs:
            if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            res = pair_try(cur, ii, jj, n, max_move, max_evals, cur_c)
            if res is not None:
                hv, hc = res
                hv = project_feasible(hv, n)
                hc = eval_c5(hv)
                if hc < cur_c - 1e-12:
                    cur = hv
                    cur_c = hc
                    improved_any = True
                    accepted += 1
                    if hc < best_c - 1e-13:
                        best_c = hc
                        best = cur.copy()
                    if accepted >= 3:
                        break

        if not improved_any:
            for _ in range(random_count):
                if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                res = pair_try(cur, ii, jj, n, max_move, max_evals, cur_c)
                if res is not None:
                    hv, hc = res
                    hv = project_feasible(hv, n)
                    hc = eval_c5(hv)
                    if hc < cur_c - 1e-12:
                        cur = hv
                        cur_c = hc
                        improved_any = True
                        if hc < best_c - 1e-13:
                            best_c = hc
                            best = cur.copy()
                        if accepted >= 3:
                            break

        if not improved_any:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp % 8 == 0:
                kick = project_feasible(best + 0.035 * rng.randn(n), n)
                ck = eval_c5(kick)
                if ck < best_c + 0.015:
                    cur = kick
                    cur_c = ck
            if no_imp > 250:
                break
        else:
            no_imp = 0

    return project_feasible(best, n), best_c


def binary_prepare_bits(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    bits = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    target = n // 2
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    bits[order[:target]] = True
    s = int(np.count_nonzero(bits))

    while s < target:
        cand = np.where(bits == 0)[0]
        if cand.size == 0:
            break
        p = cand[int(np.argmax(h[cand]))]
        bits[p] = True
        s += 1
    while s > target:
        cand = np.where(bits == 1)[0]
        if cand.size == 0:
            break
        p = cand[int(np.argmin(h[cand]))]
        bits[p] = False
        s -= 1
    return bits


def binary_anneal_bits(bits, n, deadline, rng=None, t0=0.025, cool=0.9995):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or bits is None:
        return None, None
    cur = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    cur[bits.astype(bool)] = 1.0
    target = n // 2
    s = int(np.sum(cur))
    while s < target:
        cand = np.where(cur < 0.5)[0]
        if cand.size == 0:
            break
        p = int(rng.choice(cand))
        cur[p] = 1.0
        s += 1
    while s > target:
        cand = np.where(cur > 0.5)[0]
        if cand.size == 0:
            break
        p = int(rng.choice(cand))
        cur[p] = 0.0
        s -= 1

    cur_c = eval_c5(cur)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_c = cur_c
    T = float(t0)
    last_improve = time.time()

    for _ in range(500000):
        if time.time() > deadline - 6.0:
            break
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        tries = 0
        while cur[i] == cur[j] and tries < 12:
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            tries += 1
        if cur[i] == cur[j]:
            continue

        tmp = cur[i]
        cur[i] = cur[j]
        cur[j] = tmp
        cnew = eval_c5(cur)

        if cnew < best_c - 1e-12:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_c = cnew
            last_improve = time.time()

        if cnew <= cur_c + T:
            cur_c = cnew
        else:
            tmp = cur[i]
            cur[i] = cur[j]
            cur[j] = tmp

        if T > 1e-6:
            T = max(1e-6, T * cool)

        if time.time() - last_improve > 25.0:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_c = best_c
            T = max(T, 0.012)
            for _ in range(6):
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if cur[ii] == cur[jj]:
                    continue
                tmp = cur[ii]
                cur[ii] = cur[jj]
                cur[jj] = tmp
                ck = eval_c5(cur)
                if ck < cur_c:
                    cur_c = ck
                else:
                    tmp = cur[ii]
                    cur[ii] = cur[jj]
                    cur[jj] = tmp
            last_improve = time.time()

    cur = best.copy()
    cur_c = best_c
    last_improve = time.time()
    while time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        tries = 0
        while cur[i] == cur[j] and tries < 12:
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            tries += 1
        if cur[i] == cur[j]:
            if time.time() - last_improve > 12.0:
                break
            continue
        tmp = cur[i]
        cur[i] = cur[j]
        cur[j] = tmp
        cnew = eval_c5(cur)
        if cnew < cur_c - 1e-12:
            cur_c = cnew
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cnew
                best = cur.copy()
            last_improve = time.time()
        else:
            tmp = cur[i]
            cur[i] = cur[j]
            cur[j] = tmp
        if time.time() - last_improve > 15.0:
            break

    return best, best_c


def plateau_h(n, a):
    n = int(n)
    a = float(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0))
    b = 1.0 - a
    H = 1.0 / (1.0 + a)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    if b <= 1e-12:
        h = np.full(n, H, dtype=float)
    else:
        d = np.minimum(x, 2.0 - x)
        h = np.where(d <= b, H * d / b, H)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def upsample_h(h, n, nh):
    n = int(n)
    nh = int(nh)
    if n == nh:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x_new = (np.arange(nh, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / nh)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    return project_feasible(y, nh)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1100.0
    usable = max(30.0, budget_s - 15.0)
    deadline = t0 + usable
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
        if n0 < 20:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
        n0 = 250

    if h0 is None:
        n0 = 250
        bh = None
        bc = 1e9
        for aa in (0.0, 0.15, 0.3, 0.35, 0.4, 0.5, 0.7):
            hh = plateau_h(n0, aa)
            cc = eval_c5(hh)
            if cc < bc:
                bc = cc
                bh = hh
        for _ in range(6):
            hh = project_feasible(0.5 + 0.18 * rng.randn(n0), n0)
            cc = eval_c5(hh)
            if cc < bc:
                bc = cc
                bh = hh
        h0 = bh
    else:
        if n0 % 2 == 1:
            if n0 > 20:
                h0 = h0[:n0 - 1]
                n0 -= 1
            else:
                h0 = None
                n0 = 250
        if h0 is None:
            n0 = 250
            bh = None
            bc = 1e9
            for aa in (0.0, 0.2, 0.35, 0.4, 0.5):
                hh = plateau_h(n0, aa)
                cc = eval_c5(hh)
                if cc < bc:
                    bc = cc
                    bh = hh
            h0 = bh

    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
    best_n = n0

    cand_list = []
    cand_list.append((best_h.copy(), int(best_n), float(best_c)))

    try:
        if time.time() < deadline - 40.0:
            bestp = None
            bestpc = 1e9
            for aa in (0.0, 0.15, 0.25, 0.3, 0.35, 0.4, 0.45, 0.55, 0.7):
                hh = plateau_h(best_n, aa)
                cc = eval_c5(hh)
                if cc < bestpc:
                    bestpc = cc
                    bestp = hh
            if bestp is not None and bestpc < best_c + 0.02:
                cand_list.append((bestp, int(best_n), float(bestpc)))

            for sig in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
                hh = project_feasible(best_h + sig * rng.randn(best_n), best_n)
                cc = eval_c5(hh)
                if cc < best_c + 0.02:
                    cand_list.append((hh, int(best_n), float(cc)))

            if best_n % 2 == 0:
                bb = binary_prepare_bits(best_h, best_n)
                if bb is not None:
                    hh = bb.astype(float)
                    cc = eval_c5(hh)
                    if cc < best_c + 0.03:
                        cand_list.append((hh, int(best_n), float(cc)))
    except Exception:
        pass

    main_t = cand_list[0]
    for cc in cand_list[1:]:
        if cc[2] < main_t[2]:
            main_t = cc
    main_h = np.asarray(main_t[0], dtype=float).copy()
    main_n = int(main_t[1])
    main_c = float(main_t[2])

    try:
        if time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
            dl = min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + 0.18 * usable)
            h1, c1 = top_gradient_refine(main_h, main_n, dl, rng, top_k=24, step=0.05)
            if c1 < main_c:
                main_h, main_c = h1, c1
            if main_c < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = main_h, main_c, main_n
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_c <= 0.38079:
        h, c = finalize(best_h, best_n)
        return h, c, int(best_n)

    try:
        if time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
            dl = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 0.38 * usable)
            h1, c1 = pair_local_search(main_h, main_n, dl, rng,
                                       max_move=0.25, max_evals=14, pairs_per_grad=90)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, main_n
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_c <= 0.38079:
        h, c = finalize(best_h, best_n)
        return h, c, int(best_n)

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
        try:
            bb = binary_prepare_bits(best_h, best_n)
            if bb is not None:
                dl_b = min(deadline - 50.0, time.time() + 0.22 * usable)
                hb, cb = binary_anneal_bits(bb, best_n, dl_b, rng)
                if hb is not None and cb < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, cb, best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_c <= 0.38079:
        h, c = finalize(best_h, best_n)
        return h, c, int(best_n)

    try:
        if time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
            for item in cand_list:
                if time.time() > deadline - 25.0:
                    break
                nh = int(item[1])
                if nh != best_n:
                    continue
                if item[2] > best_c + 0.02:
                    continue
                hh = np.asarray(item[0], dtype=float).copy()
                dl_c = min(deadline - 25.0, time.time() + 25.0)
                h1, c1 = pair_local_search(hh, nh, dl_c, rng,
                                           max_move=0.22, max_evals=10, pairs_per_grad=45)
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, nh
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_c <= 0.38079:
        h, c = finalize(best_h, best_n)
        return h, c, int(best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 25.0 and best_n <= 500:
        try:
            dl_p = min(deadline - 10.0, time.time() + 0.28 * max(1.0, deadline - time.time()))
            h1, c1 = pair_local_search(best_h, best_n, dl_p, rng,
                                       max_move=0.45, max_evals=12, pairs_per_grad=60)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_c <= 0.38079:
        h, c = finalize(best_h, best_n)
        return h, c, int(best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 70.0 and best_n < 500:
        try:
            nh = 500
            hu = upsample_h(best_h, best_n, nh)
            cu = eval_c5(hu)
            if cu < best_c + 0.005:
                dl1 = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 25.0)
                hu, cu = top_gradient_refine(hu, nh, dl1, rng, top_k=16, step=0.035)
                if cu < best_c + 0.005:
                    dl2 = min(deadline - 10.0, time.time() + 45.0)
                    hu, cu = pair_local_search(hu, nh, dl2, rng,
                                               max_move=0.22, max_evals=10, pairs_per_grad=35)
                    if cu < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c, best_n = hu, cu, nh
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 4.0 and best_n <= 700:
        try:
            dl_f = deadline - 4.0
            h1, c1 = pair_local_search(best_h, best_n, dl_f, rng,
                                       max_move=0.25, max_evals=10, pairs_per_grad=40)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    h, c = finalize(best_h, best_n)
    return h, c, int(best_n)
```
